# The Core Set II Art Bible

Entries for the **Core Set II** creatures (2.0 lane A): the 140 creatures in the order of `src/data/cards/core-set-2.ts`, followed by the set's one token, the Pig. The set register, the 110 spell and permanent prompts, the owner-gate register and the drafting flags stay in [core-set-2-drafts.md](core-set-2-drafts.md) until the art bible is folded (register to [index.md](index.md) section 4e, spell prompts to [spell-art.md](../spell-art.md)).

**Card facts are generated from card data** (`src/data/cards/core-set-2.ts`, the final costing of 2026-10-10 with the owner's nine fixes), so `check-art-bible` checks every fact line here. The keyword segment uses the engine's keyword ids; mechanics that are not engine keywords (Mandate, Sworn, Hunt, Tithe and the rest) stay off the fact line, as in `first-dawn.md`.

**The prompts are the drafts the art run started from.** The 250 shipped images were generated on the owner's PC, where some prompts were changed per review note. Those changed prompts are folded back in when the owner calls the art run done.

### Jia Nanfeng, the Usurper Empress — `cs2-jia-nanfeng`
- **Card facts:** {3}{B}{R} · B/R (gold frame) · 4/4 · dreaded · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Jia Nanfeng, the Jin empress who seized the court from her husband's regents, drawn as the house cast is: a historical woman, Jin by marriage and Jia Chong's daughter by blood (no family resemblance is bound to the shipped Jia Chong, so none is drawn). The card is three moments at once and the frame takes all three: she claims the Mandate by taking it (the disc torn from a rival's hand), the claim Severs a small enemy (an officer dissolving to ash-silver motes), and while she holds it her host charges (Warcry for the others). The chronicles call her short and dark, so she is drawn that way: thirties, short and compact, sturdy and full-hipped, deep olive-brown skin, thick straight brows, black hair in a low heavy chignon.
- **Personality / mood:** Dreaded: a small woman nobody blocks alone. Cold triumph, no hurry, the palace already hers.
- **Pose & composition:** Seen from a few steps back, head to knees, front three-quarter at the centre of a palace courtyard. Her right fist is raised to chest height with the crimson cord wound round it, the disc swinging just below at about y 410. A rival's hand enters from the left edge at the same height, whole and slender in a slate silk sleeve, fingers just opened and empty. At the right in the mid-ground, one rival guard is dissolving into ash-silver motes from the feet up, halberd still upright. Behind her, the halberds and blank crimson banners of her own guard surge forward, caught in the disc's light. Face about y 250, eye line y 270. Head top at or below y 179.
- **Costume & attire:** Lacquer-red empress robe over slate-teal underlayers, tarnished-silver phoenix pattern woven into the hem and cuffs (pattern only), a high collar, a wide plum-black sash, a flat tarnished-silver phoenix pin lying low in the chignon.
- **Palette:** Gold frame `#e8c95a` / `#7a5a18`, accent `#fff2b8`; B `#5a3a70` / `#140d1c`, accent `#9b6fc4`; R `#d95436` / `#5e0f0f`, accent `#f7b267`; lacquer red `#b3202a` / `#e0473c`; Jin slate-teal and tarnished silver; pale celadon jade disc with heaven-gold `#fff1b0` inner glow; ash-silver motes `#c9c6c0`.
- **Lighting:** Black's night, lit by a practical: a row of hanging palace lanterns at the right as the one lamp-amber key; a cool river-blue `#7fb3de` night rim on her left edge and along the dissolving guard.
- **Expression:** Chin lowered, a thin closed-mouth smile, eyes on the viewer as if the viewer were next.
- **Props / weapon:** The jade Mandate disc on its crimson cord (the secondary canon element), a short jian sheathed at her hip; the rival's empty hand; the Severed guard.
- **Background:** The inner court of the Luoyang palace at night: lacquer-red pillars, grey stone paving, lantern rows, a dark roofline against a deep blue sky.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the disc at the frame's centre is the glow the rings breathe from; keep her face and the plum sash smooth.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the usurpation in one frame, the take, the cost, the host.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY ONE adult woman in full view plus one rival woman's hand at the left edge and one rival guard dissolving at the right, historical Jin empress Jia Nanfeng, seen from a few steps back, her figure from head to knees inside the middle band, not a close-up, front three-quarter at the centre of a night palace courtyard, short and compact, sturdy and full-hipped, in her thirties, ### Lady Bian, Matriarch of Wei — `cs2-lady-bian`
- **Card facts:** {2}{W}{B} · W/B (gold frame) · 3/5 · bloodoath · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Lady Bian, Cao Cao's empress and Cao Pi's mother, the singer who became the dynasty's conscience: the legend Wei's sworn officers die for, and every death feeds her. Sworn has no reserved tell, so loyalty is the pose (three officers kneeling to her, oath cups raised). Her drain is drawn as the hall's memorial lamps, one lit for each officer already given, their warm light gathering on her. Forties, tall and full-figured, warm ivory skin, the amber eyes her son inherited, dark brown hair with first grey at the temples in a low coiled chignon.
- **Personality / mood:** Grave, warm and unbending; grief carried as authority, gratitude as a weapon.
- **Pose & composition:** Front three-quarter, knees up, standing at the head of the ancestral hall's steps at the centre, a bronze oath cup raised at shoulder height in her right hand, her left hand open toward the officers. Below her in the mid-ground, three Wei officers kneel in a row, heads bowed and lower than her waist, each lifting a small oath cup. Along the hall's side wall at the left, a row of five lit memorial lamps on plain blank tablets. Face about y 255, eye line y 275. Head top at or below y 179.
- **Costume & attire:** Ivory silk court robe under a plum-black overrobe woven with a lapis tiger-and-cloud pattern, bronze clasps at the shoulders, a wide lapis sash, a single bronze hairpin laid flat.
- **Palette:** Gold frame `#e8c95a` / `#7a5a18`, accent `#fff2b8`; W `#f2e8cf` / `#c9a84c`; B `#5a3a70`, accent `#9b6fc4`; Wei lapis and bronze `#a0692e` / `#d29a55`; imperial gold `#d4a83a` trims; lamp amber in the memorial lamps.
- **Lighting:** Low golden-hour sun through the hall's open doors behind her left shoulder as the one warm key; a cool lapis-blue dusk rim on her right side and the officers' helmets.
- **Expression:** Level, moist-eyed, unsmiling; the look of a woman accepting a debt she will collect.
- **Props / weapon:** The bronze oath cup; the three kneeling officers (the secondary canon element); the five lamps.
- **Background:** The Wei ancestral hall at Ye at dusk: dark lacquer pillars, bronze braziers, blank tiger-and-cloud banners, the sunset through open doors.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the sun through the doors just off-centre behind her shoulder is the glow; keep the plum overrobe and her face smooth, put texture in the lapis weave.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the matriarch and the oath that kills for her.

### Mulu, Queen of the Beasts — `cs2-mulu`
- **Card facts:** {4}{G}{G} · G · 5/5 · overrun · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Genderbent Mulu, King Mulu of the Romance's Bana Cave, the Nanman sorcerer who rode a war-elephant and rang a bell to call the beasts, as a queen. She is the bridge between rosters: Beastkin follow her and grow under her bell. Her secondary canon element is her mount (the Nanman War-Elephant card, "Mulu's mount", drawn to the same design) and two Tiger Beastkin running ahead of it. Thirties, very tall, broad-shouldered and heavily muscled, deep brown skin, thick black hair in many beaded braids bound low at the nape.
- **Personality / mood:** Overrun: a queen who has never once stopped for a wall. Joyful command; the jungle answers her.
- **Pose & composition:** Extreme wide shot, nothing cropped: the elephant mid-charge toward the viewer through a shallow jungle river, spray thrown up, trunk curled up at chest height. Mulu rides astride behind its head, upright, her right arm raising a bronze bell at shoulder height, reins in her left. Two Tiger Beastkin women sprint ahead at its forefeet, one at the left third and one at the right, heads lower than the elephant's. Her head is the highest point of anything in frame, at or below y 179; face about y 230.
- **Costume & attire:** Nanman war-regalia: a bronze-scale leather bodice, a hide war-skirt hung with sun-bronze discs, a short feather mantle of red and green macaw plumes across her shoulders, bead collars, bronze arm rings. The Beastkin wear hemp and leather wraps with jade beads.
- **Palette:** G `#4fa06a` / `#123a22`, accent `#a9dcae`; Nanman sun-bronze `#c07a2c` / `#e8b060`; jade `#3f9a78`; the elephant ash-grey with a sun-bronze brow plate; tiger orange and cream on the Beastkin.
- **Lighting:** Low golden-hour sun breaking through the canopy behind and slightly left as the one warm key; a cool river-blue rim off the water on the right edges.
- **Expression:** Open-mouthed laughing shout, eyes bright, mid-call.
- **Props / weapon:** The bronze bell (plain cast surface); the elephant; the two Tiger Beastkin.
- **Background:** A Nanman jungle river in the south: buttress-rooted trees, hanging vines, mist, a bronze drum on a far bank.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the canopy break behind her near centre is the glow; keep faces smooth and put texture in the scales, feathers and spray.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the whole stampede led by one queen.

### Yutu, Jade Rabbit of the Moon Palace — `cs2-yutu`
- **Card facts:** {4}{W}{U} · W/U (gold frame) · 4/4 · skyborne · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Yutu, the Jade Rabbit, queen of the Moon Court and Story Mode's third heroine (P13): the Beastkin anthem who lifts every kin she gathers and learns from each new arrival. A moon-hare with exactly three tells: two tall upright rabbit ears, white with pale pink inner ear, never lop; a round white cotton-puff tail; pale ruby-pink eyes. Late twenties, tall and slender with a soft full bust, porcelain-fair skin with a cool undertone, long silver-white hair bound low at the nape in a white-jade ring so the ears are the head top.
- **Personality / mood:** The gentle sovereign who gathers the wild ones home. Serene, luminous, certain; her welcome is the strongest thing in the frame.
- **Pose & composition:** Skyborne: floating a body-length above a white-jade terrace, feet clear of the stone, front three-quarter with her hips turned so the puff tail shows behind her left hip (y 470). Left hand lowered open-palmed toward the court beneath; right arm cradles the jade pestle at her shoulder. Face ≈ y 320, eye line y 305; ear tips at or below y 179, upright and slightly back-swept. The full moon is centred behind her from shoulder to waist (≈ y 400). Below her (y 560-640), exactly three Beastkin look up: two moon-hare attendants and one otter of Lanlan's crossing the white-jade bridge, the newest arrival.
- **Costume & attire:** Layered pale silk hanfu in moon white-jade and pale moon blue, deep cross-collar, long trailing sleeves with silver-thread cloud borders, a white-jade pectoral of linked discs, two long pale silk sashes streaming in the air around her.
- **Palette:** Gold `#e8c95a` / `#7a5a18`, accent `#fff2b8` as trim; moon white-jade `#eef6f2` / `#c9e3d8`; pale moon blue `#bcd6f2` / `#8fb4e0`; imperial gold `#d4a83a` fittings; river blue `#2f6fa8` night sky; fur naturals on the attendants.
- **Lighting:** Key: the full moon behind her, pale moon blue, pouring over ears, shoulders and sleeves and thrown back onto her face off the white jade. Rim: warm lantern amber from palace lanterns at the right edge.
- **Expression:** A soft closed-lip smile, eyes lowered to the court, welcoming.
- **Props / weapon:** The white-jade pestle; the jade mortar of the elixir on the terrace below, a thread of pale vapour rising (the secondary canon element).
- **Background:** The Moon Palace at night: white-jade halls with silver-gilt roofs, a silver-leaved cassia tree at left, the bridge over a pale jade stream, cloud below the terrace edge.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the centred full moon is the glow the rings breathe from; her face stays bright and off-centre, the silk textured with silver thread.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the queen aloft, her court gathering beneath her, the elixir's mortar as the canon secondary element.

### Hebe, Cupbearer of Olympus — `cs2-hebe`
- **Card facts:** {2}{W}{W} · W · 3/5 · sentinel · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Hebe as-is, cupbearer of the gods, who pours the ambrosia at Olympus's table. She replaces the base set's Hestia as white's UR and is the life-gain decks' Sworn enabler: her arrival pours 4 life, and at Sunset, if life was gained, she marks a creature you control. The art draws both in one sunset: the cup filled, and the drinker already marked. Mid-twenties and plainly adult, soft full-figured build, warm ivory-peach skin, honey-blonde curls bound low at the nape with a gilt fillet and small white blossoms, warm brown-gold eyes. She reads divine by the cloud-terrace and the sunset, never by scale. Kept apart from `gk-hestia`: no veil, no hearth, no brazier.
- **Personality / mood:** Generous, bright, unhurried: the host who notices whose cup is empty. Sentinel: she pours without ever leaving her watch.
- **Pose & composition:** Seen from a few steps back, head to knees inside the band. Hebe stands upright in front three-quarter, left of centre, on the top step of a marble cloud-terrace. Her right hand tips a tall gilt oinochoe and pours a ribbon of honey-amber ambrosia into a wide kylix. Exactly one adult Sworn warrior woman kneels on one knee on the step to her right and holds the cup up in both hands. The kneeler's head sits at about y 330, lower than Hebe's. Exactly one cyan pearl sits under the skin below the kneeler's left collarbone, at about y 400. Hebe's face is at about y 260, with her eye line at y 270, looking down at the drinker. Her head top, blossoms included, is at or below y 179.
- **Costume & attire:** Hebe: a white-and-gold pleated chiton belted high, a gilt meander hem border (pattern only), a short saffron-and-white himation over one shoulder, gilt armlets, sandals. The kneeler: bronze cuirass, leather pteryges, crimson cloak, dark hair bound low.
- **Palette:** W `#f2e8cf` / `#c9a84c`, accent `#fffef2`; marble white `#f4f1ea` / `#d9d3c5` terrace; imperial gold `#d4a83a` / `#f5d77a` on the jug, cup and fillet; bronze `#a0692e` cuirass; Aegean blue `#5fa3e0` high sky; ambrosia honey-amber; biolume cyan `#5ff0e0` / `#aefff6` only in the one pearl.
- **Lighting:** The key is the low setting sun from the right: warm golden-hour light raking across both women and lighting the ambrosia stream. The rim is cool Aegean blue `#5fa3e0` from the sky, on their left edges. The ambrosia's shine is lit by the sun, never by the Mandate disc's reserved heaven-gold.
- **Expression:** Hebe has a warm, attentive half-smile. The kneeler's eyes are lifted to her in gratitude.
- **Props / weapon:** The gilt oinochoe (her kit) and the kylix. The marked Sworn warrior is the secondary canon element: a knee bent to a legend, with no reserved Sworn tell; the pearl is the mark.
- **Background:** Olympus at sunset: a marble cloud-terrace above a sea of rose-and-gold cloud, a fluted colonnade receding at the left, laurel in gilt urns, the sun on the horizon just right of centre.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism. The sun just right of centre, behind the ambrosia stream, is the centred glow. Keep both faces and the cyan pearl smooth so the rings do not muddy them, and texture the cloud sea.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR box art: the card's whole loop in one sunset. Life is poured, and the mark already shines.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY TWO adult women and nothing else alive in the frame: Hebe, cupbearer goddess of Olympus, a woman in her mid-twenties with a soft full figure, warm ivory-peach skin, honey-blonde ### Hephaestus, Smith of Olympus — `cs2-hephaestus`
- **Card facts:** {3}{R}{R} · R · 4/5 · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Genderbent Hephaestus, smith of Olympus, kit-faithful: the hammer, the tongs, the anvil, the smith's low felt pilos cap and the one-shoulder exomis. Her Duty ({1}{R}: 2 damage) is drawn as the act of tending. The hammer comes down on a glowing bar, and the blow flings a spray of white-hot sparks and one red-hot rivet at a target past the frame edge. She is red's forge legend and the set's artifact lord, so the secondary canon element is one golden automaton handmaiden working the bellows. It is the register's construct: a golden-bronze woman with visible joint seams. Late thirties, tall and broad-shouldered, heavily muscled arms, a thick strong waist, deep bronze-brown skin, soot-dark hair cropped at the jaw, steel-grey eyes, and small pale healed spark-scars on her forearms (old marks, never wounds). - **Card facts:** {3}{U}{B} · U/B (gold frame) · 4/5 · ur, legendary · holo: prism

Circe makes it happen at her great loom: she draws one thread out of the weave and cuts it. That is Sever's "thread cut", and it also echoes the Duty tap's binding. Her left hand draws one shimmering violet thread taut out of the weave. Her right hand closes small bronze shears on it: the instant of cutting. At the right third, on a raised threshold step at the height of Circe's hip, exactly one adult warrior woman in bronze stands whole but dissolving from the feet up into ash-silver motes. ### Medusa, Gorgon of the Stone Garden — `cs2-medusa`
- **Card facts:** {3}{B}{G} · B/G (gold frame) · 3/5 · deathblade, wardingGaze · ssr, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Medusa, gorgon of the Stone Garden, drawn as a woman (register ruling) with exactly three stated gorgon tells. (1) Hair of living snakes, counted as one tell: about a dozen slender olive-and-jade-banded snakes hanging to her shoulders, none rising above her crown. (2) Serpent-gold eyes with vertical slit pupils. (3) A band of fine jade-green scales along her collarbones and the backs of her forearms. Nothing more: human legs, no serpent tail, no wings, no fangs. Her stone gaze is the Duty tap, drawn as a freeze, never a death, and never gore. Deathblade is one quiet implement: a small curved bronze sickle-knife at her hip, never drawn. Warding Gaze: she watches. She is B/G and in Persephone's Story pool, so asphodel and a pomegranate tree grow in her garden. Late twenties, statuesque and full-figured, pale olive skin.
- **Personality / mood:** Serene, sorrowful and patient. She does not hunt. She tends a garden that keeps whoever walks into it.
- **Pose & composition:** Seen from a few steps back, head to knees, front three-quarter, left of centre, standing on a mossy flagstone path among the statues. Her head is turned right, and her gaze rests level on exactly one adult warrior woman at the right third, frozen mid-stride. That warrior's raised bronze spear and round shield are already smooth grey stone, while her body stays whole and unchanged and a little pale stone dust drifts from her fingertips. A faint pale-gold shimmer hangs in the air between them like heat haze. It is not a beam. Medusa's face is at about y 280, eye line y 290. Her head top, snakes included, is at or below y 179. - **Lighting:** The key is the late golden-hour sun from the left, warm on her face and the statues. The rim is cool Aegean blue from the sea, on her right edges. Her eyes do not glow, so the gaze reads through her stare and the stone.
- **Expression:** Calm, level and sad, with the faintest closed-lipped smile.
- **Props / weapon:** The sheathed bronze sickle-knife and the garden's statues. The frozen warrior is the secondary canon element.
- **Background:** The Stone Garden: an overgrown walled garden on an island cliff at golden hour. Five marble statues of warrior women stand in it, whole and serene, every limb intact, with ivy and moss on them. Asphodel, a fruiting pomegranate tree, dark cypress, and the sea beyond.

- **Character & source:** A River Court ferry-runner who slips every net and reads the river ahead. Otter tells, exactly three: small round ears set low and back; one thick tapering otter tail; webbed fingers with sleek, wet-looking otter fur on the backs of the hands and forearms. Mid-twenties, compact and strong-shouldered, sun-browned skin, short sleek brown hair.
- **Personality / mood:** The fastest boat on the river and she knows it. Cheerful, cocky, eyes always downstream.
- **Pose & composition:** Poling a narrow reed ferry fast down a river, standing in the stern, three-quarter, leaning into the pole, looking far ahead toward the low sun on the horizon (Foresee on arrival). Behind her at left, a cast net thrown from the bank lands empty in the water, missing her (Untouchable). Face ≈ y 320; head top at or below y 179. The tail curls up behind her from the tailbone, tip inside the band (≈ y 480).
- **Costume & attire:** A sleeveless river-blue hemp tunic, chest covered, a jade-and-shell bead girdle, rolled trousers, bare feet.
- **Palette:** Gold `#e8c95a` / `#7a5a18`, accent `#fff2b8`; river blue `#2f6fa8` / `#7fb3de`; jade `#3f9a78`; reed gold; otter-brown fur.
- **Lighting:** Key: low golden-hour sun ahead of her at the right, on her face. Rim: cool river-blue light off the water behind her.
- **Expression:** A cocky grin, eyes narrowed against the glare.
- **Props / weapon:** The long bamboo pole; the reed ferry.
- **Background:** A broad river between green karst hills, reeds on the banks, the empty net splash.
- **Holo interaction:** Sheen: the sweep runs the wake and wet fur; keep the water textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** Super rare: speed, a miss and a far gaze in one frame.

### Echo of the Sibyl — `cs2-echo-of-the-sibyl`
- **Card facts:** {3}{U} · U · 2/3 · skyborne · sr · holo: sheen
- **Character & source:** The lingering voice of the Sibyl of Cumae, an original Spirit: a woman in her fifties, warm sand-brown skin, long loose silver hair, a kind lined face. She is opaque from the waist up and fades below into pale mist. Skyborne: she hangs in the air with no ground beneath her. Whispers (cast again from a fresh grave) is drawn per the register's Whispers row (owner ruling G5, 2026-10-09): her voice returning as a slow drift of blank laurel leaves, Apollo's leaf, leaving her lips toward a listener just past the right frame edge.
- **Personality / mood:** Intimate, amused, ancient; a secret told twice.
- **Pose & composition:** Floating at the centre of the cave's long gallery, her body leaning toward the right edge, one hand cupped beside her lips, the other trailing. Her misted lower body hangs well above the floor, with open air beneath it. From her lips a slow drift of about a dozen blank laurel leaves travels right at about y 300 toward the frame edge. Eye line about y 300, eyes sliding to the viewer; head top at or below y 179.
- **Costume & attire:** A sheer river-blue peplos, a pale himation drawn over the back of her head and lying flat, silver bangles, a thin laurel circlet low on the brow.
- **Palette:** U `#4a90d9` / `#16294f`, accent `#a8d4f7`; river blue `#2f6fa8` / `#7fb3de`; tufa gold-brown stone; imperial gold `#f5d77a` sun shafts; laurel green `#6b8f3e` / `#a9c46e` leaves.
- **Lighting:** Key: shafts of warm afternoon sun through the gallery's side openings, from the left. Rim: cool river-blue light from the cave's depths behind her.
- **Expression:** A conspiratorial half-smile, eyebrows lifted.
- **Props / weapon:** The drift of blank laurel leaves.
- **Background:** The Sibyl's gallery at Cumae: a long rock-cut trapezoidal passage of honey-coloured tufa, light falling through side openings in bars.
- **Holo interaction:** Sheen: the sweep rides the leaves and the bars of sunlight; keep the tufa textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** Super rare moment: the voice that will not stay buried.

### Hua Tuo, Divine Physician — `cs2-hua-tuo`
- **Card facts:** {2}{W} · W · 1/4 · r, legendary · holo: sheen
- **Character & source:** Genderbent Hua Tuo, the physician of legend: acupuncture, herbal anaesthetic, the Five Animals exercises. Her arrival returns a fallen creature to hand, so the moment is a soldier sitting up on a pallet as Hua Tuo draws a silver needle from her wrist. Sworn gains life at dawn, so it is dawn. Her secondary canon element is the patient brought back. Fifties but famously younger-looking, small and wiry, light warm skin with laugh lines, long silver-white hair bound low in one tail, quick dark eyes.
- **Personality / mood:** Brisk, kind, utterly unimpressed by death.

- **Costume & attire:** An undyed hemp and ivory physician's robe, sleeves tied back with a red cord, a leather roll of silver needles and a medicine gourd at her hip. The patient: a young adult soldier in plain bronze-studded leather, a clean white bandage on her upper arm.

- **Expression:** A small satisfied smile, eyes on the patient.

- **Background:** A field-hospital tent at dawn: rows of pallets fading back, herb bundles hanging from the ridge pole, steam from a clay pot.

### Themis, Divine Law — `cs2-themis`
- **Card facts:** {2}{W} · W · 1/3 · sentinel · r, legendary · holo: sheen
- **Character & source:** Themis as-is, Titan goddess of divine law: the Mandate by right. Her arrival claims the Mandate, drawn as the register's claim-on-arrival moment: she has just lifted the jade Mandate disc by its crimson cord out of the pan of her bronze balance, weighed and found hers. Sentinel: upright, watchful, unmoved. Her +0/+1 anthem shows in the shieldwomen below raising their shields to the disc's light. Greek Themis is not blindfolded (the blindfold is a later Roman image). Forties, tall, stately and full-bodied, deep brown skin, silver-streaked black hair coiled low at the nape, calm dark eyes. Titan by bearing and setting, never by size.
- **Personality / mood:** Grave, fair, immovable. The law does not hurry.
- **Pose & composition:** Seen from a few steps back, head to knees, front three-quarter, centre, standing upright on the high terrace at Delphi. Her left hand holds the bronze balance by its ring at chest height, the beam tipping as the left pan empties. Her right hand has just lifted the disc by its cord. The disc swings at mid-height, at about y 400. Her face is at about y 270, eye line y 280, on the disc. Her head top is at or below y 179. Below at the right, exactly three hoplite shieldwomen raise round shields in salute, small with distance.
- **Costume & attire:** A white-and-gold pleated peplos, an ivory himation, a gilt fillet, gilt cuffs, sandals.
- **Palette:** W `#f2e8cf` / `#c9a84c`, accent `#fffef2`; marble white `#f4f1ea` / `#d9d3c5` temple and terrace; imperial gold `#d4a83a` / `#f5d77a` trim; bronze `#a0692e` / `#d29a55` balance and shields; the jade Mandate disc in pale translucent celadon jade `#8fd3b0` with reserved heaven-gold `#fff1b0`; the cord in lacquer red `#b3202a`; laurel green `#6b8f3e` slopes.
- **Lighting:** The key is the low golden-hour sun from the left, warm apricot on her face and the bronze. The rim is cool Aegean blue `#5fa3e0` from the valley sky, on her right edges. The disc's soft inner heaven-gold glow is its own.
- **Expression:** Serene and grave, the faintest approving softness at the mouth.
- **Props / weapon:** The bronze balance, and the jade Mandate disc on its crimson cord.
- **Background:** Delphi's high temple terrace at golden hour: marble columns, the Phaedriades cliffs, an olive valley falling to the gulf, and the three saluting shieldwomen on the terrace below.
- **Holo interaction:** Sheen. The sweep crosses the pleats and the bronze pans. Keep the bright marble lightly veined so it does not white out.

### Bronze Sentry of Crete — `cs2-talos-sentry`
- **Card facts:** {3} · C · 2/5 · bulwark · r · holo: sheen
- **Character & source:** An original lesser sister of Talos, one of the bronze sentries Hephaestus cast to guard Crete's shores. Per the register she is a golden-bronze woman with visible joint seams, but built unlike the Forge automaton: short, stocky and broad-hipped, with thick limbs and a round face. A single bronze nail stops the vein at her right ankle, Talos's mark. Bulwark: immovable, a wall that only receives.
